# Nkambe Bakery Formula Comparison and Change Register

H2 companion: historical recipe content carried forward from H1. For the corrected baguette programme use BAG-H2-01 and PLAN-BAG-P/N/H in the operating volume.

Harmonized working edition H3 • 8 October 2026

This companion preserves the original workbook quantities alongside the owner-directed revision. Each L record is a distinct numerical ratio pattern, not necessarily a distinct product: it can be a historical experiment or an inconsistent scaling column. Do not manufacture directly from these tables. Use the selected working recipe or resolve the stated issue first.

Every original source stays unchanged. EDC is not butter and must not be treated or priced as margarine. Its identity, specification, permitted application, dose and purchase price remain unresolved. Keep EDC as a separate historical ingredient and hold affected formulas until it is identified. The earlier owner-workbook butter-to-margarine instruction remains separate; true dairy butter in independent recipes retains its specification. Milk tantalizer and improver remain omitted from revised recipes. Fryer charges, ambiguous milk, and unspecified additives remain held. Columns in a spreadsheet that have different ingredient ratios are recorded separately rather than silently called equivalent scales.

# Earlier handbook variants

The following original-formula summaries retain meaningful differences from the earlier generated handbooks. Source-local SRC numbers refer to S163’s own source register. They are not this edition’s S001–S179 identifiers. The instruction to remove improver overrides earlier suggestions to use it. These are research records, not instructions to add an improver.

## V01 — Agege bread and tangzhong milk bread

Source SRC03/SRC04. Agege, flour basis: flour 100%; water 55–58%; sugar 10–12%; fat 8–10%; instant yeast 1.5–2%; salt 1.5%; improver 0.5% as an unverified original entry. Use the actual improver supplier dose, not the generic percentage. Original starting method: mix 3 minutes low, 8–10 minutes faster; proof to expansion; bake near 180°C for 25–30 minutes. Piece mass, tin size, dough temperature and finished yield are missing. Retain the dense, stretchy style as a separate trial from S-B02.

Shokupan original: tangzhong flour 7% plus liquid 35%; main dough flour 93%, milk 50%, sugar 10%, butter 10%, egg 10%, instant yeast 1.5%, salt 1.2%. The roux liquid plus main milk totals 85% of flour before egg if both are additional. It is unclear whether the 50% milk includes the roux liquid. Resolve this before mixing; do not silently reduce liquid or present the formula as verified. Baking, portion and pan specifications are also absent. Softness claims are not shelf-life evidence.

## V02 — Specialist bread and pastry archive

These formula records come from SRC16 and the related SRC17/SRC19 drafts; SRC05/SRC14 later summarized them. Amounts below are original batch quantities unless percentages are stated. Butter, margarine and shortening are distinct ingredients requiring an explicit choice.

| Product | Retained formula or distinctive variant | Development decision |
|---|---|---|
| Plain butter biscuits | Flour 18 kg; powdered sugar 7.2 kg; butter/margarine 9 kg; egg 1.8 kg; milk powder 540 g; vanilla 90 g | Distinct from S-P04 coconut biscuits. Confirm fat, portion, mixing and water activity; reject six-month life claim. |
| Croissant | Flour 18 kg; water/milk 9.9 kg with split unspecified; sugar 1.98 kg; dough butter 720 g; salt 360 g; yeast 270 g; roll-in butter 4.5 kg | Other drafts use 9.72 kg water, 900 g dough fat and different roll-in percentages. Select one formula; do not combine them. Confirm lamination equipment, fat plasticity and proof conditions. |
| Pain au chocolat | Croissant base; 10–12 g chocolate per pastry in one version; two batons in another | Define baton mass and dough weight. Pilot 4 mm sheet and 8 × 12 cm rectangles only after the base is fixed. |
| Pound cake | Flour 18 kg; sugar 18 kg; fat 18 kg; egg 16.2 kg; baking powder 270 g; salt 90 g; flavour 100 ml | Another draft has 18 kg egg and 360 g baking powder. Select a fixed version, edible egg mass, tin fill and mixing method; use a creaming-capable mixer. |
| Hard dough loaf | Flour 18 kg; water 8.64 kg; sugar 1.26 kg; shortening 1.08 kg; salt 360 g; instant yeast 108 g | Other versions differ materially in water, sugar and fat. Validate dense-dough load and moulder limits; do not force tight rollers. |
| Pain aux raisins | Croissant dough; 2.5 kg raisins per 18 kg flour; pastry cream unspecified | Incomplete: define cream recipe, quantity, treatment and chilled handling. |
| Vendéenne-style braid | Flour 18 kg; sugar 3.24 kg; butter 4.5 kg; egg 5.4 kg; water 1.8 kg; salt 360 g; yeast 360 g | A 25%-butter variant, distinct from S-B06 50%-butter brioche. Regional naming needs care; validate cold fermentation and shaping. |
| Long sandwich roll | Flour 18 kg; water 10.8 kg; sugar 720 g; shortening 720 g; salt 360 g; instant yeast 144 g; malt 54 g | Define malt type and activity. Original 150 g dough piece is a pilot size, not final label mass. |
| Coconut crunch bread | Flour 18 kg; water 9.36 kg; sugar 2.7 kg; margarine 1.44 kg; coconut 1.8 kg; salt 270 g; yeast 180 g | Topping quantity absent; specify coconut moisture and topping before costing. |
| Whole-wheat loaf | Whole-wheat flour 9 kg + white flour 9 kg; water 11.7 kg; honey/molasses 900 g; oil 720 g; salt 360 g; yeast 126 g | Select honey or molasses. Optional oats/flax not quantified. Compare with V04’s 60% whole-wheat formula. |
| Garlic-herb-cheese fougasse | Flour 18 kg; water 11.16 kg; oil 1.08 kg; garlic 90 g; herbs 54 g; salt 360 g; yeast 90 g | Garlic-oil and cheese topping amounts absent. Define yeast type and topping yield. |
| Laminated sausage roll | Flour 18 kg; lamination fat 9 kg; meat filling 15 kg | Pastry liquid, seasoning and binding missing. O-P08 is a complete shortcrust alternative, not an equivalent laminated formula. |
| Brownie | Sugar 14.4 kg; ambiguous “cocoa margarine” 9 kg; cocoa 3.6 kg; egg 7.2 kg; flour 5.4 kg; baking powder 54 g; vanilla 50 ml | Identify fat and tray depth; validate center set and cooled cut yield. Do not approve from surface colour alone. |
| Pain viennoise | Flour 18 kg; milk 9.9 kg; sugar 1.44 kg; butter 1.8 kg; salt 360 g; yeast 180 g; optional chocolate 1.8 kg | Distinct from S-B07 pain au lait. Cost and label the chocolate version separately. |
| Levain boule | Flour 18 kg; water 11.7 kg; levain 3.6 kg; salt 360 g; instant yeast 36 g | Levain hydration, flour contribution and maturity missing; total hydration cannot be established. |
| Vanilla gelato | Milk 12 kg; cream 3 kg; sugar 3 kg; skim powder 750 g; glucose/dextrose 300 g; vanilla 60 g; stabilizer 90 g | Ingredient types/solids and stabilizer unidentified. Prefer S-D02; do not assume a soft-serve machine is a batch freezer. |
| Pineapple-ginger pop | Juice 14 kg; water 3.5 kg; 65°Brix syrup 2 kg; ginger 450 g; lime 150 g | Fruit composition and treatment missing. S-I01 is the more specified trial. |
| Cocoa cream pop | Milk 11 kg; water 3 kg; cocoa 1.5 kg; sugar 3.5 kg; “cornstarch/stabilizer” 120 g; vanilla 30 ml | Starch and stabilizer are not interchangeable. Prefer S-I03 or develop a separate specified starch formula. |
| Cocoa-banana gelato | Milk 10 kg; banana 4 kg; cocoa 1.5 kg; sugar 2.2 kg; cream 2 kg (SRC15) | Determine fat/solids, fruit treatment, freezing behaviour, equipment and portions. |

Baguette, Kumba, Pullman, burger buns, pain beurre and pain au lait from the same archive map to S-B01–S-B05 and S-B07. Their earlier ingredient ranges were development versions, not independent approved products. The exact original files remain identified in the source register; the complete S cards are the working specifications.

## V03 — Additional September concepts

Source SRC05/SRC14. These additions retain ideas not fully covered by the later cards.

| Product | Formula retained | What must be resolved |
|---|---|---|
| Cocoa-groundnut breakfast loaf | Bread flour 17.1 kg; roasted groundnut flour 900 g; cocoa 540 g; water or milk 10.8 kg; sugar 1.44 kg; oil 900 g; milk powder 540 g; salt 324 g; instant yeast 180 g; optional chocolate 1.8 kg | Select water or milk; test absorption, nut quality, allergens and portion yield. Initial 250–300 g dough, 190°C/18–22 minutes are trial starting points. |
| Chicken-vegetable pocket | Dough: flour 10 kg, water 5.5 kg, sugar 500 g, oil 400 g, salt 180 g, instant yeast 100 g. Filling: cooked chicken 5 kg, cabbage 1 kg, carrot 800 g, onion 800 g, oil 250 g, seasoning 100 g, breadcrumbs or cooked potato 500 g | Select binder and seasoning; quantify egg wash and cooked filling loss. Original assembly 60–70 g dough + 40 g filling; bake trial 195–205°C/15–18 minutes with verified core cooking. |
| Mango “ginger” soft serve | Milk 5.5 kg; cream 1.2 kg; sugar 1.4 kg; skim powder 600 g; dextrose/glucose 250 g; mango 1 kg; lime 50 g; stabilizer about 50 g | No ginger amount is supplied despite the name. Total is 10.25 kg before adjustments. Select dry sugar type and specified stabilizer; qualify treated fruit additions and machine compatibility. |
| Early bissap pop | Water 6.5 kg; infusion 1.5 kg; 65°Brix syrup 1.8 kg; ginger 150 g; lime 50 g; stabilizer 20–30 g | Syrup contributes only 1.17 kg sugar, so the original 18–22°Brix assertion is not supported by the listed sugars. Use S-I02 as the revised trial. |
| Shawarma baguette | Baguette 250 g; cooked chicken 80–100 g; fries 100–120 g; sauce 30 g; salad 30–40 g | A portion-range concept. Fix exact weights and costs using O-B18/O-B20/O-B22/O-B24 and S-B01 before sale. |
| Spring rolls | Cabbage 3 kg; carrot 1.2 kg; onion 700 g; optional cooked chicken/beef 2 kg; oil 250 g; garlic/ginger 120 g; seasoning/salt 120 g; optional breadcrumbs/cooked potato 350 g | Original target about 100 pieces, with 45–50 g filling each, purchased wrappers and 170–175°C frying trial. Define wrapper mass/count, split seasonings, sealing paste, absorbed oil and cooked filling yield; validate the center rather than relying on the original 3–4-minute time. |
| Focaccia slab; coconut nondairy frozen dessert; mango/passionfruit/banana fruit trio | Selection concepts without fully resolved formulas | Keep on the development list; do not imply a complete recipe was supplied. Rebalance fruit sugar/acid and coconut fat/solids separately. |

## V04 — October blueprint alternatives

Source SRC22; SRC23 is an exact duplicate. Values are original commercial batches. These preserve formula alternatives and new concepts without carrying over unsupported machine or life claims. Meat uses H01, dairy H02/H04 and fruit H03; no raw-meat massaging in the dairy tank.

| Original product | Retained ingredients | Harmonized decision |
|---|---|---|
| Milk sandwich bread | Flour 50 kg; water 28 L; milk powder 2.5 kg; margarine 2 kg; sugar 3 kg; salt 900 g; yeast 600 g | Older milk-powder alternative to O-P01/S-B04; do not assume four-day life. |
| Hard-crust baguette | Flour 50 kg; water 31 L; salt 1 kg; yeast 500 g; ascorbic acid 10 g | Improver specification and supplier dose required; S-B01 is the clearer working card. |
| Chin-chin | Flour 25 kg; sugar 6 kg; margarine 5 kg; milk powder 1.5 kg; nutmeg/vanilla 200 g combined; eggs 30; water 5 L | Weigh eggs, split flavour quantities, specify dough thickness and fry endpoint. Three-month life unsupported. |
| Dual-leavened beignets | Flour 20 kg; sugar 4 kg; yeast 400 g; baking powder 150 g; salt 200 g; water 13 L; flavour 100 ml | Distinct from yeast puff-puff and cake beignets. Fix leavener types and test; no guarantee of hollow or oil-free texture. |
| Powder-milk yogurt | Full-cream powder 5 kg; water 22 L; sugar 2 kg; YF-L811 culture 5 g; flavour 50 ml | Culture units, supplier dose, total solids and working tank volume unresolved; do not inherit S-Y culture dosing. |
| Chicken marinade | Chicken 20 kg; yogurt 2 L; oil 1 L; garlic 500 g; spices 300 g; lemon 400 ml; salt 250 g | Weigh liquids and identify spices; marination is refrigerated and does not replace cooking. |
| Yogurt-mayo sauce | Mayonnaise 5.5 L; yogurt 3.5 L; garlic 500 g; lemon 250 ml; vinegar 100 ml; salt/pepper 55 g combined | SRC26 splits seasoning into 40 g salt + 15 g pepper and adds mint. Keep refrigerated; final mass/volume unmeasured. |
| Meat-pie filling | Beef 4 kg; potato 5 kg; carrot 3 kg; onion 2 kg; starch 500 g; “seasoning/water” 6 L | Seasoning versus water unresolved. O-B09 provides a complete alternative; no unsupported 40% saving claim. |
| Pie crust | Flour 15 kg; cold margarine 7.5 kg; salt 200 g; cold water 4.5 L | Distinct fat ratio from O-B07; validate short mixing and chilled handling. |
| Koki corn | Corn mash 15 kg; palm oil 1.5 L; pepper 100 g; salt 120 g; water 2 L | Specify corn solids, grinding, wrap size and validated steaming endpoint. Do not assume scraper aeration. |
| Ndjansang chilli sauce | Pepper 5 kg; onion 2 kg; toasted ndjansang 300 g; oil 2 L; garlic/ginger 500 g combined; salt/bouillon 200 g combined | Split all combined ingredients. Hot bottling and oil separation do not establish ambient safety. |
| Coconut tarts | Coconut 6 kg; sugar syrup 4 L; vanilla 50 ml; shortcrust shells 100 | Specify syrup solids and shell mass/formula. Measure oven temperature throughout baking; residual heat is not automatically adequate. |
| Brioche poulet | Rich brioche dough 10 kg; cooked chicken filling 5 kg; egg wash 200 ml; sesame 50 g | Original assembly 80 g dough + 40 g filling gives 125 units before losses; define the dough/filling versions and actual yield. |
| Caramel topping | Sugar 10 kg; glucose 3 kg; water 4 L; cream 2 L; salt 30 g | The original 120°C caramelization claim is inadequate. Use a qualified controlled caramel method and appropriate vessel; dairy sauce is not automatically shelf-stable. |
| Sugar ring donuts | Flour 20 kg; sugar 3.5 kg; margarine 1.5 kg; yeast 350 g; water 9.5 L; nutmeg 100 g | Salt absent in original; resolve deliberate omission and formula before trial. Sugar coating unquantified. |
| Ganache rings | Donut rings 200; dark chocolate 4 kg; cream 3 L; butter 500 g | Define cream mass/fat, chocolate type, pickup and chilled handling. No all-day warm holding claim. |
| 60% whole-wheat loaf | Whole-wheat flour 30 kg; white flour 20 kg; water 33 L; brown sugar 2 kg; margarine 1.5 kg; salt 900 g; yeast 600 g | Distinct from V02 50% version. Hydration is 66%; adjust by measured absorption, not an automatic extra 10%. |
| Avocado-banana cream | Avocado 8 kg; banana 4 kg; lime 200 ml; ascorbic acid 15 g; icing sugar 2 kg | Antibrowning ingredients do not confer shelf stability. Treat as perishable with a qualified process. |
| Savoury puff-puff | Flour 15 kg; leeks 1.5 kg; onions 1 kg; yeast 300 g; salt 250 g; water 10 L | Sauté/cool vegetables under control, define batter mass/portion and fry endpoint; cooking oil allowance absent. |
| Akara | Peeled black-eyed-bean mash 12 kg; onion 1.5 kg; salt 100 g; white pepper 30 g; water 1.5 L | Specify mash solids, milling and hydration; validate cooking and aeration with suitable equipment. |

Frying or baking cannot be assumed equivalent to steamed koki. |
| Chin-chin | Flour 12 kg; sugar 3.5 kg; margarine 2.5 kg; eggs 30; nutmeg 80 g; vanilla 100 ml; water about 2.5 L | Record edible egg mass and final hydration. Life requires water-activity, packaging and microbial assessment. |
| Brioche poulet | Flour 10 kg; fat 1.5 kg; sugar 1.2 kg; yeast 120 g; salt 150 g; cooked chicken filling 6 kg | Dough liquid/egg missing. Original 80 g dough + 30 g filling differs from V04; do not blend counts. |
| Drinking yogurt | Milk powder 4.5 kg; water 22 L; sugar 2.2 kg; starter/plain yogurt 500 g; gelatin/pectin 50 g | Starter alternatives and gelling agents are not interchangeable. Sugar/additive post-treatment introduction and chilling need redesign. |
| Vinegar chilli sauce | Pepper 4 kg; onion 2 kg; garlic 500 g; ndjansang 100 g; oil 1.5 L; vinegar 1 L; salt 80 g | Vinegar acidity, final pH and thermal process unspecified; refrigerate under approved process, not an ambient claim. |
| Sweet milk loaf | Flour 15 kg; milk powder 600 g; sugar 1.5 kg; yeast 150 g; improver 30 g; water 8.5 L; margarine 800 g | Salt missing. Improver label required. Extra softness is not validated life extension. |

| Dual-leavened beignets | Flour 12 kg; sugar 2.8 kg; baking powder 100 g; yeast 80 g; vanilla 50 g; nutmeg 30 g; water 7.5 L | Compare with V04, with each leavener fixed and measurable. Salt omitted in original. |
| Caramel | Sugar 8 kg; water 2 L; glucose 1.5 kg; “cream/margarine” 2.5 L; salt 40 g | Cream and margarine are not interchangeable, and margarine volume is ambiguous. Use heat-safe equipment; adding dairy to caramel causes vigorous bubbling. |
| Avocado-banana cream | Avocado 10 kg; banana 4 kg; ascorbic acid 20 g; 65°Brix syrup 2 L; lime 300 ml | Weigh syrup and specify solids; original 48-hour colour claim is unverified and not a safety period. |

| Refrigerator pickle concept | Cucumber 5 kg; water 3 L; vinegar 2 L; sugar 250 g; salt 200 g; garlic 10 cloves | Vinegar acidity and garlic mass absent; sealed buckets are not an ambient preservation process. Use O-B23. |

| Condensed-milk burger buns | Flour 6 kg; condensed milk 800 g; water 2.8 L; butter 500 g; eggs 6; salt 90 g; instant yeast 80 g; sesame unspecified | Weigh egg and sesame/egg wash; use O-B25 as working alternative. |

| Condensed-milk sandwich loaf | Flour 10 kg; condensed milk 1.2 kg; water 5.4 L; shortening 400 g; salt 150 g; yeast 100 g | Working alternatives O-P01/O-P02; qualify pan load and finished mass. |

## V07 — Garlic sauce variants

Source SRC26. Toum original: peeled garlic 1.5 kg, oil 8 L, lemon juice 450 ml, water 250 ml, salt 70 g. Claimed 10 L yield is unmeasured; weigh inputs, specify oil, blend/emulsify using suitable equipment and determine usable output. A slow scraper is not assumed to make this emulsion. Garlic-in-oil sauce is a perishable product; no ambient storage is authorized.

Yogurt-mayo original: commercial mayonnaise 5.5 L, yogurt 3.5 L, garlic 500 g, lemon 250 ml, vinegar 100 ml, salt 40 g, white pepper 15 g and optional mint 2 tablespoons. Weigh ingredients and mint; verify the mayonnaise and yogurt supply specifications. Keep the cold preparation and service controlled. O-B20 and S-F04 offer smaller fully quantified alternatives with different flavour profiles.

## V08 — Brioche format options retained

SRC05’s extended brioche uses the same 50%-butter base concept as S-B06 and adds useful alternative shapes. A 5 kg flour batch has 11,450 g raw dough; at an assumed 2% handling loss, 11,221 g remains. Alternative allocations are 187 × 60 g with 1 g remainder, 140 × 80 g with 21 g remainder, 37 × 300 g with 121 g remainder, or 22 × 500 g with 221 g remainder. These are alternatives, not simultaneous outputs.

For a 500 g loaf, arrange five 100 g balls; for a three-strand braid, use 167 + 167 + 166 g. A brioche à tête may reserve about one fifth for its head. Trial small pieces at 170–180°C for 14–20 minutes, 300 g loaves at 165–175°C for 22–30 minutes, and 500 g loaves at 165–175°C for 30–40 minutes. Confirm crumb set and measured bake loss; the 90–94°C crumb target is a quality check, not a substitute for a validated food-safety process. Raw egg wash is applied only before baking.

Cool plain brioche completely before bagging. A frozen-quality trial at −18°C may compare two- and four-week samples; this does not establish a selling period or validate freezing raw dough. Keep butter, egg wash, refrigeration, rejects and unsold portions in the cost model.
